23 项认知任务原题阅读版

01. 指代性别一致｜语言｜Lan/anaphor_gender_agreement

样本：Lan/anaphor_gender_agreement:412｜discovery
【原题】
Adam should stun
正确答案：' himself'
错误选项：' herself'
【修改题】
Ann should stun
正确答案：' herself'
错误选项：' himself'

02. 主谓一致｜语言｜Lan/subject_verb_agreement
================================================================

样本：Lan/subject_verb_agreement:898｜discovery

正确答案：' were'

错误选项：' were'

错误选项：' describe'

04. 条件代码｜形式推理｜MD/code_conditional

样本：MD/code_conditional:674｜discovery
【原题】
What does the following code print?

```
def process(size):
    if size >= 13:
        return "true"
    else:
        return "false"

print(process(15))
```

Answer with just the printed value, no quotes or explanation.
正确答案：'true'
错误选项：'false'
【修改题】
What does the following code print?

```
def process(size):
    if size <= 13:
        return "true"
    else:
        return "false"

print(process(15))
```

Answer with just the printed value, no quotes or explanation.
正确答案：'false'
错误选项：'true'

样本：phys/physics_speed:930｜discovery

Answer: slow Context: A log is floating in a river that just entered a steep canyon. Answer: fast Context: A marble is rolling up a long rough tilted sandpaper tube. Answer:
正确答案：'slow'
错误选项：'fast'
【修改题】
What is the physical outcome in the following contexts? Context: A paper airplane was dropped from a window with no wind at all. Answer: slow Context: A log is floating in a river that just entered a steep canyon. Answer: fast Context: Ravi is jumping off a high diving board into a deep pool. Answer:
正确答案：'fast'
错误选项：'slow'

What is the physical outcome in the following contexts? Context: Zara is on a swing that was pushed from a very low point. Answer: slow Context: A package is on a steep conveyor belt with smooth rollers. Answer: fast Context: A train is on a winding mountain track with many sharp turns. Answer:
正确答案：'slow'
错误选项：'fast'

正确答案：'slow'
错误选项：'fast'

正确答案：'Kenji feels joyful'
错误选项：'Kenji feels afraid'
【修改题】
Kenji learned the building is dangerous. How does Kenji feel?
正确答案：'Kenji feels afraid'
错误选项：'Kenji feels joyful'

08. 欲望与目标｜社会推理｜ToM/desires_goals
================================================================

样本：ToM/desires_goals:279｜discovery
【原题】
Diego would rather be honest at the office. What does Diego want?
正确答案：'Diego wants to be truthful'
错误选项：'Diego wants to be liked'
【修改题】
Diego would rather be popular at the office. What does Diego want?
正确答案：'Diego wants to be liked'
错误选项：'Diego wants to be truthful'

样本：ToM/desires_goals:188｜discovery
【原题】

正确答案：'Carlos wants to instruct'
错误选项：'Carlos wants to learn'
【修改题】

正确答案：'Carlos wants to learn'
错误选项：'Carlos wants to instruct'

样本：Mem/latest_update:86｜discovery
【原题】

正确答案：' green'
错误选项：' black'
【修改题】

错误选项：' black'

正确答案：' black'
错误选项：' pink'

错误选项：' red'

14. 序列操作规则学习｜上下文学习｜Learn/operator_induction
================================================================

样本：Learn/operator_induction:18｜discovery
【原题】
Use the information below. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: G V D U -> U G V D; B L S A -> A B L S. F C W N ->
正确答案：' N F C W'
错误选项：' F N W C'
【修改题】
Use the information below. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: G V D U -> G U D V; B L S A -> B A S L. F C W N ->
正确答案：' F N W C'
错误选项：' N F C W'

样本：Learn/operator_induction:5｜discovery
【原题】
Read carefully. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: K T R D Q I -> K I Q D R T; P B L O J V -> P V J O L B. X M A C Y F ->
正确答案：' X F Y C A M'
错误选项：' F X M A C Y'
【修改题】
Read carefully. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: K T R D Q I -> I K T R D Q; P B L O J V -> V P B L O J. X M A C Y F ->
正确答案：' F X M A C Y'
错误选项：' X F Y C A M'

样本：Learn/operator_induction:47｜discovery
【原题】
Consider this case. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: F O J U M K -> U J O F K M; W A I D Q T -> D I A W T Q. C B H V X S ->
正确答案：' V H B C S X'
错误选项：' H V X S C B'
【修改题】
Consider this case. Rule: optionally reverse, then rotate left by a fixed count. Examples: F O J U M K -> J U M K F O; W A I D Q T -> I D Q T W A. C B H V X S ->
正确答案：' H V X S C B'
错误选项：' V H B C S X'

样本：Learn/operator_induction:4｜discovery

样本：Learn/operator_induction:1｜discovery

正确答案：' A G K R I B'
错误选项：' R K G A B I'

15. 概念组合方案选择｜创造相关代理｜Create/concept_blend_selection

样本：Create/concept_blend_selection:58｜discovery
【原题】
Consider this case. Device X provides air filtering. Device Y provides sound. A combined design must preserve both functions. What should it provide?
正确答案：' air filtering and sound'
错误选项：' air filtering and cooling'
【修改题】
Consider this case. Device X provides air filtering. Device Y provides cooling. A combined design must preserve both functions. What should it provide?
正确答案：' air filtering and cooling'
错误选项：' air filtering and sound'

================================================================
21. 多跳规则演绎｜推理｜Reason/deductive_chain
================================================================

样本：Reason/deductive_chain:94｜discovery
【原题】
Consider this case. Rules: All E are U. All U are B. All U are N. All N are L. All E are B. All U are M. All N are B. Item K is E. Can "K is L" be proved using only these facts? Answer yes or no.
正确答案：' yes'
错误选项：' no'
【修改题】
Consider this case. Rules: All E are U. All U are B. All U are N. All N are L. All E are B. All U are M. All N are B. Item K is Z. Can "K is L" be proved using only these facts? Answer yes or no.
正确答案：' no'
错误选项：' yes'

样本：Reason/deductive_chain:110｜discovery
【原题】

正确答案：' yes'
错误选项：' no'
【修改题】
Rules: All H are N. All D are K. All Y are J. All H are D. All S are N. All H are K. All D are S. All S are Y. All Y are K. Item K is Z. Can "K is J" be proved using only these facts? Answer yes or no.
正确答案：' no'
错误选项：' yes'

样本：Reason/causal_intervention:268｜discovery
【原题】
Read carefully. Binary rules: A=0; B=A; C=1-B; D=C XOR C; E=D XOR B. Force B=0 instead of B=A, keep A fixed, then recompute downstream variables. What is E?
正确答案：' 0'
错误选项：' 1'
【修改题】
Read carefully. Binary rules: A=0; B=A; C=1-B; D=C XOR C; E=D XOR B. Force B=1 instead of B=A, keep A fixed, then recompute downstream variables. What is E?
正确答案：' 1'
错误选项：' 0'

正确答案：' 1'
错误选项：' 0'

正确答案：' 0'
错误选项：' 1'

Binary rules: A=1; B=A; C=1-B; D=1-C; E=D AND B; F=1-E. Force B=0 instead of B=A, keep A fixed, then recompute downstream variables. What is F?
正确答案：' 1'
错误选项：' 0'
【修改题】
Binary rules: A=1; B=A; C=1-B; D=1-C; E=D AND B; F=1-E. Force B=1 instead of B=A, keep A fixed, then recompute downstream variables. What is F?
正确答案：' 0'
错误选项：' 1'
